.b369		91 08		sta ($08),y	                sta (up),y      ; so only the LSB is used.
.b36b		e8		inx		                inx
.b36c		e8		inx		                inx
.b36d		60		rts		z_set_current:  rts
.b36e						xt_set_order:
.b36e						w_set_order:
.b36e		b5 01		lda $01,x	                lda 1,x         ; just check MSB sign bit since other negative
.b370		10 0e		bpl $b380	                bpl _start      ; values have undefined behavior anyway
.b372		ca		dex		                dex             ; Make room for the count.
.b373		ca		dex		                dex
.b374		74 03		stz $03,x	                stz 3,x         ; ROOT-WORDLIST is 3
.b376		a9 03		lda #$03	                lda #3
.b378		95 02		sta $02,x	                sta 2,x
.b37a		74 01		stz $01,x	                stz 1,x         ; Count is 1.
.b37c		a9 01		lda #$01	                lda #1
.b37e		95 00		sta $00,x	                sta 0,x
.b380						_start:
.b380		a0 22		ldy #$22	                ldy #num_order_offset
.b382		e8		inx		                inx             ; Pre-drop the count so we can preserve Z flag
.b383		e8		inx		                inx
.b384		b5 fe		lda $fe,x	                lda $fe,x
.b386		91 08		sta ($08),y	                sta (up),y      ; #ORDER is a byte variable.
.b388		85 26		sta $26		                sta tmp1        ; Save a copy for zero check and looping.
.b38a		f0 0d		beq $b399	                beq _done       ; If zero, there are no wordlists.
.b38c		a0 23		ldy #$23	                ldy #search_order_offset        ; offset to start of byte array
.b38e						_loop:
.b38e		b5 00		lda $00,x	                lda 0,x         ; The search order is a byte array
.b390		91 08		sta ($08),y	                sta (up),y      ; so only save the LSB
.b392		c8		iny		                iny
.b393		e8		inx		                inx
.b394		e8		inx		                inx
.b395		c6 26		dec $26		                dec tmp1
.b397		d0 f5		bne $b38e	                bne _loop
.b399						_done:
.b399		60		rts		z_set_order:    rts
.b39a						xt_to_order:
.b39a						w_to_order:
.b39a		20 7a 93	jsr $937a	                jsr w_to_r
.b39d		20 74 b2	jsr $b274	                jsr w_get_order
.b3a0		20 52 8e	jsr $8e52	                jsr w_r_from
.b3a3		20 45 92	jsr $9245	                jsr w_swap
.b3a6		20 62 8c	jsr $8c62	                jsr w_one_plus
.b3a9		20 6e b3	jsr $b36e	                jsr w_set_order
.b3ac		60		rts		z_to_order:     rts
.b3ad						xt_wordlist:
.b3ad						w_wordlist:
.b3ad		a0 09		ldy #$09	                ldy #num_wordlists_offset
.b3af		b1 08		lda ($08),y	                lda (up),y      ; This is a byte variable, so only
.b3b1		c9 0c		cmp #$0c	                cmp #max_wordlists
.b3b3		d0 05		bne $b3ba	                bne _ok
.b3b5		a9 0b		lda #$0b	                lda #err_wordlist
.b3b7		4c 55 cf	jmp $cf55	                jmp error
.b3ba						_ok:
.b3ba		1a		inc a		                ina             ; Increment the wordlist#
.b3bb		91 08		sta ($08),y	                sta (up),y      ; Save it into byte variable #wordlists
.b3bd		ca		dex		                dex             ; and put it on the stack.
.b3be		ca		dex		                dex
.b3bf		95 00		sta $00,x	                sta 0,x
.b3c1		74 01		stz $01,x	                stz 1,x         ; 12 is the max, so upper byte is always zero.
.b3c3		60		rts		z_wordlist:     rts

;******  Processing file: platform/../definitions.asm

=$0000						ram_start = $0000          ; start of installed RAM, must include zpage
=$0000						zpage     = ram_start      ; begin of Zero Page ($0000-$00ff)
=$0100						stack0    = $0100          ; begin of Return Stack ($0100-$01ff)
=$7f						zpage_end = $7F            ; end of Zero Page used ($0000-$007f)
=32767						ram_end   = $8000-1        ; end of installed RAM
=31744						hist_buff = ram_end-$03ff  ; begin of history buffers
=$ff						rsp0      = $ff              ; initial Return Stack Pointer (65c02 stack)
=$ff						bsize     = $ff              ; size of input/output buffers
=$0000						user0     = zpage            ; TaliForth2 system variables
=512						buffer0   = stack0+$100      ; input buffer ($0200-$02ff)
=768						cp0       = buffer0+bsize+1  ; Dictionary starts after last buffer
=31744						cp_end    = hist_buff        ; Last RAM byte available for code
=$ff						padoffset = $ff              ; offset from CP to PAD (holds number strings)
=120						dsp0      = zpage_end-7    ; initial Data Stack Pointer
.b3c4						cold_zp_table:
>b3c4	0000	00 08				cp:         .word cp0+256+1024      ; Compiler Pointer
>b3c6	0002	04 c1				dp:         .word dictionary_start  ; Dictionary Pointer
>b3c8	0004	00 00				ip:         .word 0                 ; Instruction Pointer (current xt)
>b3ca	0006	00 00				workword:   .word 0                 ; nt (not xt!) of word being compiled, except in
>b3cc	0008	00 03				up:         .word cp0               ; Forth user vars at start of available RAM
>b3ce	000a	00 00				insrc:      .word 0                 ; input source for SOURCE-ID (0 for keyboard)
>b3d0	000c	00 02				cib:        .word buffer0           ; address of current input buffer
>b3d2	000e	00 00				ciblen:     .word 0                 ; length of current input buffer
>b3d4	0010	00 00				toin:       .word 0                 ; pointer to CIB (>IN in Forth)
>b3d6	0012	22 f0				output:     .word kernel_putc       ; vector for EMIT
>b3d8	0014	26 f0				input:      .word kernel_getc       ; vector for KEY
>b3da	0016	34 f0				havekey:    .word kernel_kbhit      ; vector for KEY?
>b3dc	0018	0a 00				base:       .word 10                ; number radix, default decimal
>b3de	001a	00 00				state:      .word 0                 ; STATE: -1 compile, 0 interpret
>b3e0	001c	00 00				status:     .word 0                 ; internal status used by CREATE : :NONAME ; ACCEPT NUMBER
>b3e2	001e	00				cache_valid:    .byte 0
>b3e3	001f					tmpdsp:     .byte ?         ; temporary DSP (X) storage (single byte)
>b3e4	0020					loopctrl:   .byte ?         ; Offset from lcbstack0 to current loop control block for DO/LOOP/+LOOP
>b3e5	0021					loopidx0    .byte ?         ; cached LSB of current loop index for LOOP (not +LOOP)
=$0100						lcbstack0 = stack0
=256						loopindex = lcbstack0+0     ; loop control block index for adjusted loopindex
=258						loopfufa  = lcbstack0+2     ; loop control block offset for limit fudge factor
>b3e6	0022					loopleave:  .word ?         ; tmp for LEAVE chaining ;TODO could it use existing tmp?
>b3e8	0024					tmptos:     .word ?         ; temporary TOS storage
>b3ea	0026					tmp1:       .word ?         ; temporary storage
>b3ec	0028					tmp2:       .word ?         ; temporary storage
>b3ee	002a					tmp3:       .word ?         ; temporary storage (especially for print)
>b3f0	002c					tohold:     .word ?         ; pointer for formatted output
>b3f2	002e					scratch:    .word ?,?,?,?   ; 8 byte scratchpad (see UM/MOD)
>b3fa	0036					tmped:      .word ?,?,?     ; temporary for editors
.b3e3						cold_zp_table_end:
.b3e3						cold_user_table:

>b40e	002b	00

>b413	0030	00 00				buffstatus_offset:      .word 0         ; Buffer status (bit 0 = used, bit 1 = dirty) (not in use)
>b415	0032	05 b0				blockread_offset:       .word xt_block_word_error   ; Vector to block reading routine
>b417	0034	05 b0				blockwrite_offset:      .word xt_block_word_error   ; Vector to block writing routine

>cd17		01				    .byte %0000_0001
>cd18		02				    .byte %0000_0010
>cd19		04				    .byte %0000_0100

.cd25						bloom_common:
.cd25		a1 02		lda ($02,x)	        lda (2,x)
.cd27		6a		ror a		        ror                     ; C = parity of first character
.cd28		08		php		        php                     ; preserve V and C flags

.cd30						_loop:

.cd32		26 2e		rol $2e		        rol fnv_hash            ; take next 3 bits from fnv_hash, rolling in parity bit on first pass

.cd35		26 2e		rol $2e		        rol fnv_hash
.cd37		2a		rol a		        rol
.cd38		26 2e		rol $2e		        rol fnv_hash

.cd41		50 08		bvc $cd4b	        bvc _test               ; set or test?

.cd4b		39 00 7b	and $7b00,y	        and bloom_bits,y        ; test whether target bit is set
